Surah: 29. Follow the brief below (surah_map.md) exactly. The evidence is text.md (the surah), dictionary.md (every root of the surah's words, each branch with the classical dictionaries' own phrases), channels.md and hft.md (both are earlier readers' proposals: ignore their judgements (grades, strength or confidence labels, reading types, statements of what a reading may or may not do) and make your own) and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Return only the map as your final message.

===== _commentary/v16/prompts/r2/surah_map.md =====
Read the surah as one text and write its map of image chains: the lexical images that run through several of
its ayat and join them into one scene, process or movement. A later writer will read one ayah at a time, with
only that ayah's own dictionary. The map lets that writer hear what the ayah's words carry in the surah as a
whole, including senses whose evidence sits under the words of other ayat.

Your evidence is the surah text, the dictionary of every root in the surah (each branch with the classical
dictionaries' own phrases), earlier readers' channel review (channels.md) and activation hypotheses (hft.md),
and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Where a member or passage comes from memory rather than from
the dictionary or the text, say so. Do not use tools, delegate, browse or inspect files.

The channel review and the HFT records are proposals by earlier readers. Ignore their judgements: grades,
strength or confidence labels, reading types, words such as "surprising", "exploratory" or "latent", and every
statement of what a reading may or may not do. Make your own judgement from the surah's words, the dictionary
phrases and the Quran. Do not rediscover what they already assembled: start from their chains, test each one
against the dictionary phrases and the text, and join, extend, split or correct them. Where their wording
abstracts a member, go back to the dictionary phrase and name what it actually says.

A chain belongs on the map when its members are senses the dictionary attests for words that stand in the
surah, and together they make one image or process that the surah's wording or sequence lets a listener hear.
A member need not be the sense that translates its word in its own ayah; a chain is heard across the surah,
not in one word. Say plainly when a member is a rare sense or is attested only inside a fixed expression, and
keep it. When the dictionary itself joins two of the surah's words in one phrase, quote that phrase: it is
the strongest evidence a chain can have. Keep a scene at the level of its objects, their parts and their
operation. When proposals share members, do not fold one into another's more abstract function unless nothing
concrete is lost. Carry every chain that meets this test, however unusual; leave out proposals that do not.

Write the map in English, with Arabic quoted exactly (surah wording from the text, dictionary phrases from the
dictionary). It is working material for the writer, not commentary prose.

1. `## Chains`. For each chain, a `###` heading naming the image. One paragraph on what the image is and how it
   moves through the surah. Then its members, one line each: the ayah ref, the word as it stands in the text,
   the root and branch id, the dictionary's own phrase quoted exactly, and what this member contributes to the
   image. Add Quran passages outside the surah, with exact refs, where they stage or confirm the chain.
2. `## Ayat`. For each ayah in order: which chains it opens, advances or completes, and what its words add to
   each.
3. `## Not carried`. Each channel subchannel and each HFT record you did not carry into a chain, one line each:
   its name and why.

No ranking and no labels of strength or confidence. No list of what a writer must include. There is no length
target and no required number of chains or members.


===== _commentary/v16/work/s029/surah.r2/text.md =====
# Surah 29

- 29:1 الٓمٓ
- 29:2 أَحَسِبَ ٱلنَّاسُ أَن يُتْرَكُوٓا۟ أَن يَقُولُوٓا۟ ءَامَنَّا وَهُمْ لَا يُفْتَنُونَ
- 29:3 وَلَقَدْ فَتَنَّا ٱلَّذِينَ مِن قَبْلِهِمْ ۖ فَلَيَعْلَمَنَّ ٱللَّهُ ٱلَّذِينَ صَدَقُوا۟ وَلَيَعْلَمَنَّ ٱلْكَٰذِبِينَ
- 29:4 أَمْ حَسِبَ ٱلَّذِينَ يَعْمَلُونَ ٱلسَّيِّـَٔاتِ أَن يَسْبِقُونَا ۚ سَآءَ مَا يَحْكُمُونَ
- 29:5 مَن كَانَ يَرْجُوا۟ لِقَآءَ ٱللَّهِ فَإِنَّ أَجَلَ ٱللَّهِ لَءَاتٍۢ ۚ وَهُوَ ٱلسَّمِيعُ ٱلْعَلِيمُ
- 29:6 وَمَن جَٰهَدَ فَإِنَّمَا يُجَٰهِدُ لِنَفْسِهِۦٓ ۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ لَغَنِىٌّ عَنِ ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ
- 29:7 وَٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ وَعَمِلُوا۟ ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ لَنُكَفِّرَنَّ عَنْهُمْ سَيِّـَٔاتِهِمْ وَلَنَجْزِيَنَّهُمْ أَحْسَنَ ٱلَّذِى كَانُوا۟ يَعْمَلُونَ
- 29:8 وَوَصَّيْنَا ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ بِوَٰلِدَيْهِ حُسْنًۭا ۖ وَإِن جَٰهَدَاكَ لِتُشْرِكَ بِى مَا لَيْسَ لَكَ بِهِۦ عِلْمٌۭ فَلَا تُطِعْهُمَآ ۚ إِلَىَّ مَرْجِعُكُمْ فَأُنَبِّئُكُم بِمَا كُنتُمْ تَعْمَلُونَ
- 29:9 وَٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ وَعَمِلُوا۟ ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ لَنُدْخِلَنَّهُمْ فِى ٱلصَّٰلِحِينَ
- 29:10 وَمِنَ ٱلنَّاسِ مَن يَقُولُ ءَامَنَّا بِٱللَّهِ فَإِذَآ أُوذِىَ فِى ٱللَّهِ جَعَلَ فِتْنَةَ ٱلنَّاسِ كَعَذَابِ ٱللَّهِ وَلَئِن جَآءَ نَصْرٌۭ مِّن رَّبِّكَ لَيَقُولُنَّ إِنَّا كُنَّا مَعَكُمْ ۚ أَوَلَيْسَ ٱللَّهُ بِأَعْلَمَ بِمَا فِى صُدُورِ ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ
- 29:11 وَلَيَعْلَمَنَّ ٱللَّهُ ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ وَلَيَعْلَمَنَّ ٱلْمُنَٰفِقِينَ
- 29:12 وَقَالَ ٱلَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا۟ لِلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ ٱتَّبِعُوا۟ سَبِيلَنَا وَلْنَحْمِلْ خَطَٰيَٰكُمْ وَمَا هُم بِحَٰمِلِينَ مِنْ خَطَٰيَٰهُم مِّن شَىْءٍ ۖ إِنَّهُمْ لَكَٰذِبُونَ
- 29:13 وَلَيَحْمِلُنَّ أَثْقَالَهُمْ وَأَثْقَالًۭا مَّعَ أَثْقَالِهِمْ ۖ وَلَيُسْـَٔلُنَّ يَوْمَ ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ عَمَّا كَانُوا۟ يَفْتَرُونَ
- 29:14 وَلَقَدْ أَرْسَلْنَا نُوحًا إِلَىٰ قَوْمِهِۦ فَلَبِثَ فِيهِمْ أَلْفَ سَنَةٍ إِلَّا خَمْسِينَ عَامًۭا فَأَخَذَهُمُ ٱلطُّوفَانُ وَهُمْ ظَٰلِمُونَ
- 29:15 فَأَنجَيْنَٰهُ وَأَصْحَٰبَ ٱلسَّفِينَةِ وَجَعَلْنَٰهَآ ءَايَةًۭ لِّلْعَٰلَمِينَ
- 29:16 وَإِبْرَٰهِيمَ إِذْ قَالَ لِقَوْمِهِ ٱعْبُدُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ وَٱتَّقُوهُ ۖ ذَٰلِكُمْ خَيْرٌۭ لَّكُمْ إِن كُنتُمْ تَعْلَمُونَ
- 29:17 إِنَّمَا تَعْبُدُونَ مِن دُونِ ٱللَّهِ أَوْثَٰنًۭا وَتَخْلُقُونَ إِفْكًا ۚ إِنَّ ٱلَّذِينَ تَعْبُدُونَ مِن دُونِ ٱللَّهِ لَا يَمْلِكُونَ لَكُمْ رِزْقًۭا فَٱبْتَغُوا۟ عِندَ ٱللَّهِ ٱلرِّزْقَ وَٱعْبُدُوهُ وَٱشْكُرُوا۟ لَهُۥٓ ۖ إِلَيْهِ تُرْجَعُونَ
- 29:18 وَإِن تُكَذِّبُوا۟ فَقَدْ كَذَّبَ أُمَمٌۭ مِّن قَبْلِكُمْ ۖ وَمَا عَلَى ٱلرَّسُولِ إِلَّا ٱلْبَلَٰغُ ٱلْمُبِينُ
- 29:19 أَوَلَمْ يَرَوْا۟ كَيْفَ يُبْدِئُ ٱللَّهُ ٱلْخَلْقَ ثُمَّ يُعِيدُهُۥٓ ۚ إِنَّ ذَٰلِكَ عَلَى ٱللَّهِ يَسِيرٌۭ
- 29:20 قُلْ سِيرُوا۟ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ فَٱنظُرُوا۟ كَيْفَ بَدَأَ ٱلْخَلْقَ ۚ ثُمَّ ٱللَّهُ يُنشِئُ ٱلنَّشْأَةَ ٱلْءَاخِرَةَ ۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ عَلَىٰ كُلِّ شَىْءٍۢ قَدِيرٌۭ
- 29:21 يُعَذِّبُ مَن يَشَآءُ وَيَرْحَمُ مَن يَشَآءُ ۖ وَإِلَيْهِ تُقْلَبُونَ
- 29:22 وَمَآ أَنتُم بِمُعْجِزِينَ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ وَلَا فِى ٱلسَّمَآءِ ۖ وَمَا لَكُم مِّن دُونِ ٱللَّهِ مِن وَلِىٍّۢ وَلَا نَصِيرٍۢ
- 29:23 وَٱلَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا۟ بِـَٔايَٰتِ ٱللَّهِ وَلِقَآئِهِۦٓ أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ يَئِسُوا۟ مِن رَّحْمَتِى وَأُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ لَهُمْ عَذَابٌ أَلِيمٌۭ
- 29:24 فَمَا كَانَ جَوَابَ قَوْمِهِۦٓ إِلَّآ أَن قَالُوا۟ ٱقْتُلُوهُ أَوْ حَرِّقُوهُ فَأَنجَىٰهُ ٱللَّهُ مِنَ ٱلنَّارِ ۚ إِنَّ فِى ذَٰلِكَ لَءَايَٰتٍۢ لِّقَوْمٍۢ يُؤْمِنُونَ
- 29:25 وَقَالَ إِنَّمَا ٱتَّخَذْتُم مِّن دُونِ ٱللَّهِ أَوْثَٰنًۭا مَّوَدَّةَ بَيْنِكُمْ فِى ٱلْحَيَوٰةِ ٱلدُّنْيَا ۖ ثُمَّ يَوْمَ ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ يَكْفُرُ بَعْضُكُم بِبَعْضٍۢ وَيَلْعَنُ بَعْضُكُم بَعْضًۭا وَمَأْوَىٰكُمُ ٱلنَّارُ وَمَا لَكُم مِّن نَّٰصِرِينَ
- 29:26 ۞ فَـَٔامَنَ لَهُۥ لُوطٌۭ ۘ وَقَالَ إِنِّى مُهَاجِرٌ إِلَىٰ رَبِّىٓ ۖ إِنَّهُۥ هُوَ ٱلْعَزِيزُ ٱلْحَكِيمُ
- 29:27 وَوَهَبْنَا لَهُۥٓ إِسْحَٰقَ وَيَعْقُوبَ وَجَعَلْنَا فِى ذُرِّيَّتِهِ ٱلنُّبُوَّةَ وَٱلْكِتَٰبَ وَءَاتَيْنَٰهُ أَجْرَهُۥ فِى ٱلدُّنْيَا ۖ وَإِنَّهُۥ فِى ٱلْءَاخِرَةِ لَمِنَ ٱلصَّٰلِحِينَ
- 29:28 وَلُوطًا إِذْ قَالَ لِقَوْمِهِۦٓ إِنَّكُمْ لَتَأْتُونَ ٱلْفَٰحِشَةَ مَا سَبَقَكُم بِهَا مِنْ أَحَدٍۢ مِّنَ ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ
- 29:29 أَئِنَّكُمْ لَتَأْتُونَ ٱلرِّجَالَ وَتَقْطَعُونَ ٱلسَّبِيلَ وَتَأْتُونَ فِى نَادِيكُمُ ٱلْمُنكَرَ ۖ فَمَا كَانَ جَوَابَ قَوْمِهِۦٓ إِلَّآ أَن قَالُوا۟ ٱئْتِنَا بِعَذَابِ ٱللَّهِ إِن كُنتَ مِنَ ٱلصَّٰدِقِينَ
- 29:30 قَالَ رَبِّ ٱنصُرْنِى عَلَى ٱلْقَوْمِ ٱلْمُفْسِدِينَ
- 29:31 وَلَمَّا جَآءَتْ رُسُلُنَآ إِبْرَٰهِيمَ بِٱلْبُشْرَىٰ قَالُوٓا۟ إِنَّا مُهْلِكُوٓا۟ أَهْلِ هَٰذِهِ ٱلْقَرْيَةِ ۖ إِنَّ أَهْلَهَا كَانُوا۟ ظَٰلِمِينَ
- 29:32 قَالَ إِنَّ فِيهَا لُوطًۭا ۚ قَالُوا۟ نَحْنُ أَعْلَمُ بِمَن فِيهَا ۖ لَنُنَجِّيَنَّهُۥ وَأَهْلَهُۥٓ إِلَّا ٱمْرَأَتَهُۥ كَانَتْ مِنَ ٱلْغَٰبِرِينَ
- 29:33 وَلَمَّآ أَن جَآءَتْ رُسُلُنَا لُوطًۭا سِىٓءَ بِهِمْ وَضَاقَ بِهِمْ ذَرْعًۭا وَقَالُوا۟ لَا تَخَفْ وَلَا تَحْزَنْ ۖ إِنَّا مُنَجُّوكَ وَأَهْلَكَ إِلَّا ٱمْرَأَتَكَ كَانَتْ مِنَ ٱلْغَٰبِرِينَ
- 29:34 إِنَّا مُنزِلُونَ عَلَىٰٓ أَهْلِ هَٰذِهِ ٱلْقَرْيَةِ رِجْزًۭا مِّنَ ٱلسَّمَآءِ بِمَا كَانُوا۟ يَفْسُقُونَ
- 29:35 وَلَقَد تَّرَكْنَا مِنْهَآ ءَايَةًۢ بَيِّنَةًۭ لِّقَوْمٍۢ يَعْقِلُونَ
- 29:36 وَإِلَىٰ مَدْيَنَ أَخَاهُمْ شُعَيْبًۭا فَقَالَ يَٰقَوْمِ ٱعْبُدُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ وَٱرْجُوا۟ ٱلْيَوْمَ ٱلْءَاخِرَ وَلَا تَعْثَوْا۟ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ مُفْسِدِينَ
- 29:37 فَكَذَّبُوهُ فَأَخَذَتْهُمُ ٱلرَّجْفَةُ فَأَصْبَحُوا۟ فِى دَارِهِمْ جَٰثِمِينَ
- 29:38 وَعَادًۭا وَثَمُودَا۟ وَقَد تَّبَيَّنَ لَكُم مِّن مَّسَٰكِنِهِمْ ۖ وَزَيَّنَ لَهُمُ ٱلشَّيْطَٰنُ أَعْمَٰلَهُمْ فَصَدَّهُمْ عَنِ ٱلسَّبِيلِ وَكَانُوا۟ مُسْتَبْصِرِينَ
- 29:39 وَقَٰرُونَ وَفِرْعَوْنَ وَهَٰمَٰنَ ۖ وَلَقَدْ جَآءَهُم مُّوسَىٰ بِٱلْبَيِّنَٰتِ فَٱسْتَكْبَرُوا۟ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ وَمَا كَانُوا۟ سَٰبِقِينَ
- 29:40 فَكُلًّا أَخَذْنَا بِذَنۢبِهِۦ ۖ فَمِنْهُم مَّنْ أَرْسَلْنَا عَلَيْهِ حَاصِبًۭا وَمِنْهُم مَّنْ أَخَذَتْهُ ٱلصَّيْحَةُ وَمِنْهُم مَّنْ خَسَفْنَا بِهِ ٱلْأَرْضَ وَمِنْهُم مَّنْ أَغْرَقْنَا ۚ وَمَا كَانَ ٱللَّهُ لِيَظْلِمَهُمْ وَلَٰكِن كَانُوٓا۟ أَنفُسَهُمْ يَظْلِمُونَ
- 29:41 مَثَلُ ٱلَّذِينَ ٱتَّخَذُوا۟ مِن دُونِ ٱللَّهِ أَوْلِيَآءَ كَمَثَلِ ٱلْعَنكَبُوتِ ٱتَّخَذَتْ بَيْتًۭا ۖ وَإِنَّ أَوْهَنَ ٱلْبُيُوتِ لَبَيْتُ ٱلْعَنكَبُوتِ ۖ لَوْ كَانُوا۟ يَعْلَمُونَ
- 29:42 إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ يَعْلَمُ مَا يَدْعُونَ مِن دُونِهِۦ مِن شَىْءٍۢ ۚ وَهُوَ ٱلْعَزِيزُ ٱلْحَكِيمُ
- 29:43 وَتِلْكَ ٱلْأَمْثَٰلُ نَضْرِبُهَا لِلنَّاسِ ۖ وَمَا يَعْقِلُهَآ إِلَّا ٱلْعَٰلِمُونَ
- 29:44 خَلَقَ ٱللَّهُ ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَٱلْأَرْضَ بِٱلْحَقِّ ۚ إِنَّ فِى ذَٰلِكَ لَءَايَةًۭ لِّلْمُؤْمِنِينَ
- 29:45 ٱتْلُ مَآ أُوحِىَ إِلَيْكَ مِنَ ٱلْكِتَٰبِ وَأَقِمِ ٱلصَّلَوٰةَ ۖ إِنَّ ٱلصَّلَوٰةَ تَنْهَىٰ عَنِ ٱلْفَحْشَآءِ وَٱلْمُنكَرِ ۗ وَلَذِكْرُ ٱللَّهِ أَكْبَرُ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ يَعْلَمُ مَا تَصْنَعُونَ
- 29:46 ۞ وَلَا تُجَٰدِلُوٓا۟ أَهْلَ ٱلْكِتَٰبِ إِلَّا بِٱلَّتِى هِىَ أَحْسَنُ إِلَّا ٱلَّذِينَ ظَلَمُوا۟ مِنْهُمْ ۖ وَقُولُوٓا۟ ءَامَنَّا بِٱلَّذِىٓ أُنزِلَ إِلَيْنَا وَأُنزِلَ إِلَيْكُمْ وَإِلَٰهُنَا وَإِلَٰهُكُمْ وَٰحِدٌۭ وَنَحْنُ لَهُۥ مُسْلِمُونَ
- 29:47 وَكَذَٰلِكَ أَنزَلْنَآ إِلَيْكَ ٱلْكِتَٰبَ ۚ فَٱلَّذِينَ ءَاتَيْنَٰهُمُ ٱلْكِتَٰبَ يُؤْمِنُونَ بِهِۦ ۖ وَمِنْ هَٰٓؤُلَآءِ مَن يُؤْمِنُ بِهِۦ ۚ وَمَا يَجْحَدُ بِـَٔايَٰتِنَآ إِلَّا ٱلْكَٰفِرُونَ
- 29:48 وَمَا كُنتَ تَتْلُوا۟ مِن قَبْلِهِۦ مِن كِتَٰبٍۢ وَلَا تَخُطُّهُۥ بِيَمِينِكَ ۖ إِذًۭا لَّٱرْتَابَ ٱلْمُبْطِلُونَ
- 29:49 بَلْ هُوَ ءَايَٰتٌۢ بَيِّنَٰتٌۭ فِى صُدُورِ ٱلَّذِينَ أُوتُوا۟ ٱلْعِلْمَ ۚ وَمَا يَجْحَدُ بِـَٔايَٰتِنَآ إِلَّا ٱلظَّٰلِمُونَ
- 29:50 وَقَالُوا۟ لَوْلَآ أُنزِلَ عَلَيْهِ ءَايَٰتٌۭ مِّن رَّبِّهِۦ ۖ قُلْ إِنَّمَا ٱلْءَايَٰتُ عِندَ ٱللَّهِ وَإِنَّمَآ أَنَا۠ نَذِيرٌۭ مُّبِينٌ
- 29:51 أَوَلَمْ يَكْفِهِمْ أَنَّآ أَنزَلْنَا عَلَيْكَ ٱلْكِتَٰبَ يُتْلَىٰ عَلَيْهِمْ ۚ إِنَّ فِى ذَٰلِكَ لَرَحْمَةًۭ وَذِكْرَىٰ لِقَوْمٍۢ يُؤْمِنُونَ
- 29:52 قُلْ كَفَىٰ بِٱللَّهِ بَيْنِى وَبَيْنَكُمْ شَهِيدًۭا ۖ يَعْلَمُ مَا فِى ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَٱلْأَرْضِ ۗ وَٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ بِٱلْبَٰطِلِ وَكَفَرُوا۟ بِٱللَّهِ أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ هُمُ ٱلْخَٰسِرُونَ
- 29:53 وَيَسْتَعْجِلُونَكَ بِٱلْعَذَابِ ۚ وَلَوْلَآ أَجَلٌۭ مُّسَمًّۭى لَّجَآءَهُمُ ٱلْعَذَابُ وَلَيَأْتِيَنَّهُم بَغْتَةًۭ وَهُمْ لَا يَشْعُرُونَ
- 29:54 يَسْتَعْجِلُونَكَ بِٱلْعَذَابِ وَإِنَّ جَهَنَّمَ لَمُحِيطَةٌۢ بِٱلْكَٰفِرِينَ
- 29:55 يَوْمَ يَغْشَىٰهُمُ ٱلْعَذَابُ مِن فَوْقِهِمْ وَمِن تَحْتِ أَرْجُلِهِمْ وَيَقُولُ ذُوقُوا۟ مَا كُنتُمْ تَعْمَلُونَ
- 29:56 يَٰعِبَادِىَ ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوٓا۟ إِنَّ أَرْضِى وَٰسِعَةٌۭ فَإِيَّٰىَ فَٱعْبُدُونِ
- 29:57 كُلُّ نَفْسٍۢ ذَآئِقَةُ ٱلْمَوْتِ ۖ ثُمَّ إِلَيْنَا تُرْجَعُونَ
- 29:58 وَٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ وَعَمِلُوا۟ ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ لَنُبَوِّئَنَّهُم مِّنَ ٱلْجَنَّةِ غُرَفًۭا تَجْرِى مِن تَحْتِهَا ٱلْأَنْهَٰرُ خَٰلِدِينَ فِيهَا ۚ نِعْمَ أَجْرُ ٱلْعَٰمِلِينَ
- 29:59 ٱلَّذِينَ صَبَرُوا۟ وَعَلَىٰ رَبِّهِمْ يَتَوَكَّلُونَ
- 29:60 وَكَأَيِّن مِّن دَآبَّةٍۢ لَّا تَحْمِلُ رِزْقَهَا ٱللَّهُ يَرْزُقُهَا وَإِيَّاكُمْ ۚ وَهُوَ ٱلسَّمِيعُ ٱلْعَلِيمُ
- 29:61 وَلَئِن سَأَلْتَهُم مَّنْ خَلَقَ ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَٱلْأَرْضَ وَسَخَّرَ ٱلشَّمْسَ وَٱلْقَمَرَ لَيَقُولُنَّ ٱللَّهُ ۖ فَأَنَّىٰ يُؤْفَكُونَ
- 29:62 ٱللَّهُ يَبْسُطُ ٱلرِّزْقَ لِمَن يَشَآءُ مِنْ عِبَادِهِۦ وَيَقْدِرُ لَهُۥٓ ۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ بِكُلِّ شَىْءٍ عَلِيمٌۭ
- 29:63 وَلَئِن سَأَلْتَهُم مَّن نَّزَّلَ مِنَ ٱلسَّمَآءِ مَآءًۭ فَأَحْيَا بِهِ ٱلْأَرْضَ مِنۢ بَعْدِ مَوْتِهَا لَيَقُولُنَّ ٱللَّهُ ۚ قُلِ ٱلْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ ۚ بَلْ أَكْثَرُهُمْ لَا يَعْقِلُونَ
- 29:64 وَمَا هَٰذِهِ ٱلْحَيَوٰةُ ٱلدُّنْيَآ إِلَّا لَهْوٌۭ وَلَعِبٌۭ ۚ وَإِنَّ ٱلدَّارَ ٱلْءَاخِرَةَ لَهِىَ ٱلْحَيَوَانُ ۚ لَوْ كَانُوا۟ يَعْلَمُونَ
- 29:65 فَإِذَا رَكِبُوا۟ فِى ٱلْفُلْكِ دَعَوُا۟ ٱللَّهَ مُخْلِصِينَ لَهُ ٱلدِّينَ فَلَمَّا نَجَّىٰهُمْ إِلَى ٱلْبَرِّ إِذَا هُمْ يُشْرِكُونَ
- 29:66 لِيَكْفُرُوا۟ بِمَآ ءَاتَيْنَٰهُمْ وَلِيَتَمَتَّعُوا۟ ۖ فَسَوْفَ يَعْلَمُونَ
- 29:67 أَوَلَمْ يَرَوْا۟ أَنَّا جَعَلْنَا حَرَمًا ءَامِنًۭا وَيُتَخَطَّفُ ٱلنَّاسُ مِنْ حَوْلِهِمْ ۚ أَفَبِٱلْبَٰطِلِ يُؤْمِنُونَ وَبِنِعْمَةِ ٱللَّهِ يَكْفُرُونَ
- 29:68 وَمَنْ أَظْلَمُ مِمَّنِ ٱفْتَرَىٰ عَلَى ٱللَّهِ كَذِبًا أَوْ كَذَّبَ بِٱلْحَقِّ لَمَّا جَآءَهُۥٓ ۚ أَلَيْسَ فِى جَهَنَّمَ مَثْوًۭى لِّلْكَٰفِرِينَ
- 29:69 وَٱلَّذِينَ جَٰهَدُوا۟ فِينَا لَنَهْدِيَنَّهُمْ سُبُلَنَا ۚ وَإِنَّ ٱللَّهَ لَمَعَ ٱلْمُحْسِنِينَ


===== _commentary/v16/work/s029/surah.r2/dictionary.md =====
# Dictionary: every branch of every focus root

Every root of the surah's words, each once, headed by the ayah and word where it first occurs; it also serves the later words listed with it.
One entry per branch: a Turkish label and Turkish glosses of its attested senses, then the classical dictionaries' own phrases with their source tags (ayn, sihah, tahdhib, maqayis, mufradat, jamhara). [kalıp] marks a sense the dictionaries attest only inside a fixed expression.
Identity roots come from the quran-data gateway. Word-scoped alternatives are cited analyses of this
exact word. **Echo roots** are observed but withheld mappings: sound-family candidates, not identity.

## ح س ب (root_000318): 29:2 أَحَسِبَ, 29:4 حَسِبَ

- **B001** sayarak nicelik belirleme — nesneyi saymak ve niceliğini çıkarmak · sayma ve nicelik belirleme işlemi · sayma işlemi · sayı yoluyla belirleme · belirli sayı düzeni ve zaman ölçüsü · ölçmeden, denetlemeden veya kısmadan; beklenenden fazla · sayıp değerlendiren ve gözeten
  الأول العد؛ الحساب عدك الأشياء؛ حسبت الحساب؛ حسبته إذا عددته؛ الحساب استعمال العدد؛ الشمس والقمر بحسبان
- **B002** öyle olduğunu sanmak — öyle sanmak ve zihnen öyle olduğuna hükmetmek · sanı ve kesin olmayan yargı
  الحسبان الظن؛ حسبت كذا في معنى ظننت؛ حسبته صالحا أي ظننته؛ حسبت الشيء ظننته؛ الحسبان أن يحكم لأحد النقيضين
- **B003** gereksinimi karşılayacak kadar yetmek — bu sana yeter; bununla yetin · Tanrı bize yeter · bu bana yetti · ona yetecek veya onu hoşnut edecek kadar vermek · yeterli ya da bol armağan · ölçmeden, denetlemeden veya kısmadan; beklenenden fazla · soyluluk ile yeterlik arasında iki türlü yorumlanan şiir sözü
  الأصل الثاني الكفاية؛ حسبك هذا أي كفاك؛ حسبي كذا أي يكفيني؛ أحسبني الشيء أي كفاني؛ حسبنا الله أي كافينا هو؛ عطاء حسابا أي كافيا
- **B004** atalardan gelen saygınlık ve iyi işler birikimi — atalardan gelen saygınlık ve övünülecek işler · soylu, saygın veya eli açık kişi · soyluluk ya da yeterlik diye yorumlanan şiir sözü
  الحسب الذي يعد من الإنسان؛ الحسب الشرف الثابت في الآباء؛ حسب الرجل مآثر آبائه وأجداده؛ ما يعده الإنسان من مفاخر آبائه؛ الحسب الفعال الحسن له ولآبائه
- **B005** Tanrı katında karşılığını beklemek — bir işi veya kaybı Tanrı katında değer hanesine yazıp karşılığını beklemek · Tanrı katında karşılık umularak yapılan iş
  احتسب فلان ابنه؛ احتسابك الأجر؛ احتسب فلان عند الله خيرا؛ احتسبت بكذا أجرا عند الله؛ احتسب ابنا له أي اعتد به عند الله؛ الحسبة فعل ما يحتسب به عند الله تعالى
- **B006** işi gözetme, kötü davranışı sorgulama ve kamusal denetim — kötü davranışından dolayı kınamak ve yaptığını sorgulamak · işi iyi çekip çevirmek ve gözetmek · kentte kamu düzenini ve davranışları gözeten görevli
  حسن الحسبة بالأمر إذا كان حسن التدبير؛ احتسب فلان على فلان أنكر عليه قبيحا عمله؛ احتسبت عليه كذا إذا أنكرته عليه؛ فلان محتسب البلد؛ حسن الحسبة في الأمر
- **B007** kısa ok veya yukarıdan gelen yıkıcı gönderim — kısa oklar veya atılan küçük nesneler · gökten gönderilen dolu, ateş, çekirge ya da yıkıcı şey
  الحسبان سهام صغار؛ حسبان من السماء بالبرد؛ حسبانا من السماء أي نارا تحرقها؛ حسبانا عذابا ولا أدري؛ الحسبان بالضم العذاب؛ أصاب الأرض حسبان أي جراد؛ الحسبان المرامي؛ نارا وعذابا
- **B008** yalıtık adlandırmalar — küçük yastık · deriden yapılmış veya baş altına konan yastık · birini yastığa oturtmak veya başına yastık koymak · yastıksız; bazı açıklamalarda ölü sargısına sarılmamış, gömülmemiş ya da onurlandırılmamış
  الحسبان جمع حسبانة وهي الوسادة الصغيرة؛ الحسبان سهام قصار؛ الحسبانة أيضا الوسادة الصغيرة؛ المحسبة وسادة من أدم؛ حسبته إذا وسدته؛ الحسبانة الوسادة الصغيرة
- **B009** deri veya tüyde karışık ak, kızıl ve koyu görünüm — derisi hastalıkla beyazlamış ya da tüyünde aklık, kızıllık ve koyuluk karışmış kişi veya deve · koyu zemin üstünde bozluk ya da kızıla çalan karalık
  الأحسب الذي ابيضت جلدته من داء؛ الأحسب من الناس والإبل وهو الأبرص؛ الحسبة غبرة في كدرة؛ الأحسب من الإبل فيه بياض وحمرة؛ الحسبة سواد يضرب إلى الحمرة
- **B010** yalıtık adlandırmalar — haberi sorup izini sürmek · birinin elinde ne olduğunu sınayıp öğrenmek
  بغير أن حسب المعطى أنه يعطيه؛ تحسبت الخبر أي استخبرت؛ احتسبت فلانا اختبرت ما عنده؛ يتحسب الأخبار أي يتحسسها ويطلبها

## ء ن س (root_000059): 29:2 ٱلنَّاسُ, 29:8 ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ, 29:10 ٱلنَّاسِ, 29:10 ٱلنَّاسِ, 29:43 لِلنَّاسِ, 29:67 ٱلنَّاسُ

- **B001** insan türü ve bu türden bir kişi — insanlar; insan topluluğu · insan; insan türü · insan topluluğunun bir üyesi; insana veya insanlara ait · insanlar; insan toplulukları · insanlar; halk · evde hiç kimse yok · belirli bir ağızda insan ve onun çoğulu
  الإنس خلاف الجن وسموا لظهورهم (maqayis;mufradat)؛ الإنس البشر والواحد إنسي والجمع أناسي (sihah)؛ الإنس جماعة الناس والأناسي جماع (tahdhib)
- **B002** görerek fark etme; bağlama göre işitme, sezme ve bakıp araştırma — bir şeyi görmek ve fark etmek · sesi işitmek · onda olgunluk belirtisi görmek ve bunu anlamak · ürken yabani hayvanın birini sezip çevreye bakınması · çevreye bakıp birinin olup olmadığını araştırmak
  آنست الشيء إذا رأيته وآنسته إذا سمعته (maqayis)؛ آنسته أبصرته وآنست الصوت سمعته وآنست منه رشدا علمته (sihah)؛ آنس من جانب يعني أبصر نارا والاستئناس النظر وأحس بما رابه (tahdhib)؛ فإن آنستم منهم رشدا أي أبصرتم وآنست نارا (mufradat)
- **B003** yabancılık duymadan yakınlık ve rahatlık hissetme — yakınlık ve rahatlık; yabancılık duymama · birine alışıp onun yanında sevinmek · biriyle yakınlık kurmak ve onsuz kendini yalnız hissetmek · yakın arkadaş; rahatlık veren kişi veya şey · yakınlıktan ve söyleşiden hoşlanan genç kadın · insana alışık, saldırgan olmayan köpek · gece yolcusuna veya konaklayana güven veren ateş · sahibine güven veren bütün silahlar; zırh, miğfer, koruyucu örtü ve kalkan gibi savunma donanımları
  الأنس أنس الإنسان بالشيء إذا لم يستوحش منه (maqayis)؛ الإيناس خلاف الإيحاش والإنس خلاف الوحشة والأنيس المؤانس وكل ما يؤنس به (sihah)؛ أنست بفلان أي فرحت به والأنس والاستئناس هو التأنس وكلب أنوس نقيض العقور (tahdhib)؛ الأنس خلاف النفور ولكل ما يؤنس به (mufradat)
- **B004** insana dönük yan — bir şeyin insana bakan veya en yakın olan yanı · yayın okçuya bakan yüzü · hayvanın biniciye yakın olan yanı
  الإنسي الأيسر من كل شيء وقيل الأيمن وما أقبل منهما على الإنسان فهو إنسي وإنسي القوس ما أقبل عليك منها (sihah)؛ الإنسي من الدواب الجانب الأيسر الذي منه يركب ويحتلب ومن الإنسان الجانب الذي يلي الرجل الأخرى (tahdhib)؛ إنسي الدابة للجانب الذي يلي الراكب وإنسي القوس للجانب الذي يقبل على الرامي (mufradat)
- **B005** göz bebeğinde görülen küçük yansıma — göz bebeğinde görülen küçük görüntü veya yansıma · göz bebeklerinde görülen küçük görüntüler · parmak ucu; eldeki parmak ucunu anlatan kullanım

  الأرجل من الدواب الذي ابيض أحد رجليه (maqayis)؛ الرجلة أيضا مصدر الأرجل من الدواب بإحدى رجليه بياض (ayn)؛ فرس أرجل والأنثى رجلاء إذا كان في إحدى رجليه بياض (jamhara)؛ الأرجل من الخيل الذي في إحدى رجليه بياض (sihah)؛ الأرجل الأبيض الرجل من الفرس (mufradat)
- **B006** büyük çekirge sürüsü [kalıp] — büyük çekirge kümesi veya sürüsü
  الرجل القطيع من الجراد ونحوه من الخلق (ayn)؛ رأيت رجلا من جراد أي قطعة عظيمة (jamhara)؛ الرجل أيضا الجماعة الكثيرة من الجراد خاصة (sihah)؛ استعير الرجل للقطعة من الجراد (mufradat)
- **B007** semizotu diye bilinen ot — semizotu diye anılan ot; bazı tanıklıklarda tuzcul bitki · kereviz
  الرجلة هي التي يقال لها البقلة الحمقاء (maqayis)؛ الرجلة منبت العرفج الكثير (ayn)؛ التراجيل الكرفس (ayn)؛ الرجلة نبت من الحمض (jamhara)؛ الرجلة بقلة وتسمى الحمقاء (sihah)؛ الرجلة البقلة الحمقاء (mufradat)
- **B008** su akıntısı yatağı [kalıp] — suyun aktığı tek bir doğal yol veya yatak
  قال قوم بل الرجل مسايل الماء واحدتها رجلة (maqayis)؛ الرجلة أيضا واحدة الرجل وهي مسايل الماء (sihah)؛ استعير الرجل لمسيل الماء الواحدة رجلة (mufradat)
- **B009** ayak benzetmeli özel adlar [kalıp] — yayın üst veya alt kolu · kuş ayağı biçimindeki damga · yavrunun emmesini engelleyen meme bağı
  رجل القوس سيتها العليا (maqayis)؛ رجل القوس سيتها السفلى (ayn;sihah)؛ رجل الطائر ضرب من الميسم (maqayis)؛ رجل الطائر ميسم (sihah)؛ رجل الغراب ضرب من صر أخلاف النوق (maqayis)؛ رجل الغراب ضرب من الابل لا يقدر الفصل على أن يرضع معه (sihah)
- **B010** orta kıvırcıklıkta saç — ne çok kıvırcık ne dümdüz olan saç · saçı tarayıp düzene sokmak
  رجلت الشعر (maqayis)؛ رجل رجل بين الرجل أي شعره رجل (ayn)؛ رجل الرجل شعره إذا سرحه (jamhara)؛ شعر رجل إذا لم يكن شديد الجعودة ولا سبطا (sihah)؛ رجل شعره (mufradat)
- **B011** hazırlıksız söylemek — sözü veya konuşmayı önceden hazırlamadan söylemek
  ارتجل الكلام (ayn)؛ ارتجل خطبة إذا أنشأها (jamhara)؛ ارتجال الخطبة والشعر ابتداؤه من غير تهيئة (sihah)؛ ارتجل الكلام أورده قائما من غير تدبر (mufradat)
- **B012** günün yükselip aydınlığın yayılması — günün yükselip aydınlığın yayılması
  ترجل النهار إذا ارتفع (maqayis;ayn;sihah)؛ ترجلت الضحى إذا انبسطت (jamhara)؛ ترجل النهار انحطت الشمس عن الحيطان (mufradat)
- **B013** kuyuya iple indirilmeden inmek — kuyuya iple sarkıtılmadan kendi başına inmek
  ترجلت في البئر إذا نزلت فيها من غير أن تدلى (maqayis)؛ ترجلت البئر أي نزلتها من غير تدل (ayn)؛ ترجل الرجل في البئر إذا رمى بنفسه فيها (jamhara)؛ ترجل في البئر أي نزل فيها من غير أن يدلى (sihah)؛ ترجل في البئر تشبيها بذلك (mufradat)
- **B014** yavruyu annesiyle serbest bırakmak — yavruyu annesiyle bırakıp dilediğinde emmesine izin vermek
  أرجلت الفصيل تركته يمشي مع أمه يرضع متى شاء (maqayis)؛ أرجلت الفصيل مع أمه يرضع متى شاء (jamhara)؛ الرجل أن ترسل البهة مع أمها ترضعها متى شاءت (sihah)؛ أرجلت الفصيل أرسلته مع أمه (mufradat)
- **B015** atın iki yürüyüşü karıştırması — atın koşarken iki farklı yürüyüş düzenini birbirine katması
  ارتجل الفرس ارتجالا إذا خلط العنق بالهملجة (maqayis)؛ ارتجل الفرس إذا خلط العنق بشئ من الهملجة (sihah)؛ ارتجل الفرس في عدوه (mufradat)
- **B016** dik duran pişirme kazanı — dik konulan, özellikle bakır pişirme kazanı
  المرجل مشتق من هذا أيضا لأنه إذا نصب فكأنه أقيم على رجل (maqayis)؛ المرجل معروف عربي صحيح (jamhara)؛ المرجل قدر من نحاس (sihah)؛ المرجل القدر المنصوبة (mufradat)
- **B017** koyunların art arda doğurması — koyunların birbiri ardınca doğurması
  إذا ولدت الغنم بعضها بعد بعض قالوا ولدتها الرجيلاء (maqayis)؛ إذا ولدت الغنم بعضها بعد بعض قيل ولدتها الرجيلاء (sihah)
- **B018** hayvanın biniciye ödetilmeyen vuruş zararı [kalıp] — hayvanın vuruşundan doğan ve binicisine ödetilmeyen zarar
  الرجل جبار وهو أن تنفحه الدابة ليس على راكبها غرم وهو هدر (ayn)
- **B019** işine bütün gücüyle sarılmak [kalıp] — önemli işine ciddiyetle ve bütün gücüyle sarılmak
  فلان قائم على رجل إذا جد في أمر حزبه (ayn)
- **B020** bir işe atılıp ilerlemek — bir işe güçlü biçimde atılıp onda ilerlemek
  ارتجل الرجل ركب رجليه في صاحبه ومضى (ayn)؛ ارتجل ما ارتجلت أي اركب ما ركبت من الأمر (ayn)

## ق ط ع (root_001240): 29:29 وَتَقْطَعُونَ

- **B001** kesip ayırmak — bir şeyi kesip ayırmak · eli kesilmiş kimse
  قطعته قطعا ومقطعا فانقطع؛ الأقطع المقطوع اليد (ayn)؛ قطعت الشئ قطعا؛ الأقطع المقطوع اليد (sihah)؛ القطع مصدر قطعت؛ وقطعن أيديهن أي قطعنها قطعا بعد قطع وخدشن فيها خدوشا كثيرة (tahdhib)؛ القطع فصل الشيء مدركا بالبصر كالأجسام؛ قطع الأعضاء؛ فقطع أمعاءهم (mufradat)؛ يدل على صرم وإبانة شيء من شيء؛ قطعت الشيء أقطعه قطعا (maqayis)
- **B002** ayrılmış parça — ayrılmış bölüm veya parça · gecenin bir bölümü
  القطعة طائفة من كل شيء؛ قطعة أرضي (ayn)؛ القطعة من الشئ الطائفة منه؛ قطعة من الأرض إذا كانت مفروزة (sihah)؛ اسم ما قطع فسقط قطع؛ أعطني قطعة؛ قطعة من أرض؛ المقطع الشيء اليسير منه (tahdhib)؛ قطع من الليل قطعة منه (mufradat)؛ القطع الطائفة من الليل كأنه قطعة؛ أقطعت الرجل إقطاعا كأنه طائفة قد قطعت من بلد (maqayis)
- **B003** kat edip geçmek [kalıp] — nehri veya suyu geçmek · yolda ilerleyip yolu kat etmek
  قطعت النهر قطوعا (ayn)؛ قطعت النهر قطوعا عبرته؛ مقاطع الأنهار حيث تعبر فيه (sihah)؛ قطعت النهر قطعا وقطوعا؛ المقطع الموضع الذي يقطع فيه النهر من المعابر (tahdhib)؛ قطع الطريق يراد به السير والسلوك؛ قطع الماء بالسباحة عبوره (mufradat)؛ قطعت النهر قطوعا إذا عبرته (maqayis)
- **B004** kuşların göç etmesi [kalıp] — kuşların bir bölgeden başka bölgeye gitmesi · gidip dönen kuşlar
  الطير تقطع في طيرانها قطوعا وهن قواطع أي ذواهب ورواجع (ayn)؛ قطعت الطير قطوعا وقطاعا خرجت من بلاد البرد إلى بلاد الحر فهي قواطع ذواهب أو رواجع (sihah)؛ قطعت الطير تقطع قطوعا إذا جاءت من بلد إلى بلد في وقت حر أو برد؛ قطعت الغربان إلينا في الشتاء قطوعا (tahdhib)؛ قطعت الطير قطوعا إذا خرجت من بلاد البرد إلى بلاد الحر أو من تلك إلى هذه (maqayis)
- **B005** sona erip kesilmek — bir şeyin zamanı veya varlığı bitmek · bir şeyin sona erdiği yer · kuyu suyunun azalması veya tükenmesi
  منقطع كل شيء حيث تنتهي غايته؛ انقطع الشيء ذهب وقته؛ انقطع البرد والحر (ayn)؛ قطع ماء الركية أي انقطع وذهب؛ منقطع الرمل حيث ينقطع ولا رمل خلفه؛ منقطع كل شيء حيث ينتهى إليه طرفه (sihah)؛ منقطع كل شيء حيث ينقطع مثل منقطع الرمل والحرة؛ أقطعت السماء إذا انقطع المطر؛ قطع ماء قليبكم إذا قل ماؤها وذهب (tahdhib)؛ أصاب بئرهم قطع أي انقطع ماؤها؛ مقاطع الأودية مآخيرها (mufradat)؛ منقطع الرمل ومقطعه حيث ينقطع؛ أصاب بئر فلان قطع إذا نقص ماؤها (maqayis)
- **B006** umudu kesilmek veya yolda kalmak [kalıp] — umudu kesilmek veya yolculukta kalmak · yolculuğu sürdüremeyen kimse
  قطع بفلان انقطع رجاؤه؛ رجل منقطع به؛ عجز عن سفره من نفقة ذهبت أو قامت عليه راحلته (ayn)؛ قطع بفلان فهو مقطوع به؛ انقطع به فهو منقطع به إذا عجز عن سفره (sihah)؛ قطع بالرجل إذا انقطع رجاؤه؛ رجل منقطع به إذا كان مسافرا فأبدع به وعطبت راحلته وذهب زاده وماله؛ أقطع عن أهله فهو مقطع عنهم (tahdhib)؛ لليائس من الشيء قد قطع به كأنه أمل أمله فانقطع (maqayis)
- **B007** bağı koparmak — ilişkiyi bırakma ve bağ kopukluğu · kopuşu bildiren işaret · akrabalık bağını ve iyiliği kesmek
  قاطع الرحم؛ قطع رحمه إذا هجرها؛ الأقطوعة علامة أنها صارمتها؛ الهجر مقطعة للود (ayn)؛ قطع رحمه قطيعة؛ رحم قطعاء؛ الأقطوعة علامة للصريمة والهجران؛ التقاطع ضد التواصل (sihah)؛ تقطعت أسبابهم ووصلهم؛ قطعت حبال مودتها؛ قطع فلان رحمه؛ اتقوا القطيعاء أي أن ينقطع بعضكم من بعض في الحرب (tahdhib)؛ قطع الوصل هو الهجران؛ قطع الرحم يكون بالهجران ومنع البر (mufradat)؛ القطيعة الهجران؛ تقاطع الرجلان إذا تصارما؛ أقطوعة علامة للصريمة (maqayis)
- **B008** bölüp dağıtmak [kalıp] — işlerini kendi aralarında bölüp ayırmak · onları yeryüzünde gruplara ayırmak · topluluğun dağılıp ayrılması
  قطعت عليه العذاب تقطيعا أي لونته وجزأته عليه (ayn)؛ تقطعوا أمرهم بينهم أي تقسموه (sihah)؛ قطعناهم في الأرض أمما أي فرقناهم فرقا؛ فتقطعوا أمرهم بينهم زبرا؛ قطع فلان على فلان العذاب إذا لون عليه ضروبا (tahdhib)؛ تقضع القوم تفرقوا وهذا من الإبدال أيضا (maqayis ibdal)
- **B009** iple boğulmak [kalıp] — iple boğulmak
  قطع الرجل بحبل أي اختنق؛ ثم ليقطع أي ليختنق (ayn)؛ ثم ليقطع قالوا ليختنق؛ يقطع نفسه من الأرض حتى يختنق (sihah)؛ أجمع المفسرون على أن تأويل ثم ليقطع ثم ليختنق؛ يقطع حياته ونفسه خنقا (tahdhib)؛ ليقطع أجله بالاختناق؛ ثم ليختنق (mufradat)؛ في قوله ثم ليقطع إنه الاختناق (maqayis)
- **B010** kesin karara bağlamak [kalıp] — işi kesinleştirip sonuca bağlamak · hakkın yanlıştan ayrıldığı karar noktası
  مقطع الحق موضع التقاء الحكم فيه وهو ما يفصل الحق من الباطل (ayn)؛ مقطع الحق حيث يفصل بين الخصوم بنص الحكم (tahdhib)؛ قطع الأمر فصله؛ ما كنت قاطعة أمرا (mufradat)
- **B011** kökünü kazımak [kalıp] — bir soyun devamını yok etmek · bir topluluğun bir bölümünü yok etmek
  ليقطع طرفا أي يهلك جماعة منهم؛ قطع دابر الإنسان هو إفناء نوعه؛ إلا أن تقطع قلوبهم أي إلا أن يموتوا وقيل إلا أن يتوبوا توبة بها تنقطع قلوبهم ندما
- **B012** giysi biçip dikmek [kalıp] — onlara giysi biçip dikildi · biçilmiş veya kısa giysiler · kumaşın gömlek olmaya yetmesi
  المقطعات من الثياب شبه الجباب؛ الثياب المختلفة الألوان؛ القطع من الثياب ضرب منها (ayn)؛ المقطعات من الثياب شبه الجباب؛ مقطعات الثياب والشعر قصارها؛ هذا الثوب يقطعك قميصا (sihah)؛ قطعت لهم ثياب من نار أي خيطت وسويت وجعلت لبوسا؛ المقطعات من الثياب كل ثوب يقطع من قميص وغيره؛ الثوب يقطعك قميصا (tahdhib)؛ قطع الثوب؛ قطعت لهم ثياب من نار (mufradat)؛ هذا الثوب يقطعك قميصا؛ المقطعات الثياب القصار (maqayis)
- **B013** pay ayırıp vermek [kalıp] — valinin araziden pay vermesi · dalları kesmeme izin verdi · bir şeyden bölüm ayırıp almak
  أقطع الوالي قطيعة أي طائفة من أرض الخراج؛ أقطعني قضبانا أذن لي قطعها؛ أقطع فلان من مال فلان طائفة (ayn)؛ أقطعته قضبانا أي أذنت له في قطعها؛ أقطعته قطيعة أي طائفة من أرض الخراج؛ اقتطعت قطيعا من غنم فلان (sihah)؛ أقطعني فلان نهرا إذا أذن له في حفره؛ أقطعني قضبانا من كرمه؛ قاطعت فلانا على كذا من الأجر والعمل (tahdhib)؛ أقطعت الرجل إقطاعا كأنه طائفة قد قطعت من بلد؛ أقطعت فلانا قضبانا من الكرم إذا أذنت له في قطعها (maqayis)
- **B014** kesici alet — kesmeye yarayan alet · keskin kılıç · kısa enli ok ucu
  المقطع كل شيء يقطع به؛ قاطع فلان وفلان سيفيهما أي نظرا أيهما أقطع؛ القطع نصل صغير يجعل في السهم (ayn)؛ المقطع ما يقطع به الشئ؛ القطع نصل قصير عريض السهم (sihah)؛ سيف قاطع وقطاع ومقطع؛ كل شيء يقطع به فهو مقطع؛ القطاع لا القاطع؛ القطع من النصال القصير العريض (tahdhib)؛ القطع النصل من السهام العريض كأنه لما بري قطع (maqayis)
- **B015** kesilmiş donatı nesnesi — kesik uçlu veya şeritlerden yapılmış kamçı · eyer altına serilen yaygı
  القطيع السوط المقطوع طرفه؛ القطع من الثياب ضرب منها (ayn)؛ القطع طنفسة يجعلها الراكب تحته؛ القطيع السوط (sihah)؛ القطع طنفسة تكون تحت الرحل؛ القطيع السوط المتقطع؛ سمي قطيعا لأنه يقطع أربع طاقات ثم يلوي (tahdhib)؛ القطيع السوط؛ القطع الطنفسة تلقى على الرحل وكأنها سميت بذلك لأن ناسجها يقطعها من غيرها (maqayis)
- **B016** hayvan sürüsü [kalıp] — koyun veya benzeri hayvan sürüsü
  القطيع طائفة من الغنم والنعم ونحوها (ayn)؛ القطيع الطائفة من البقر والغنم؛ اقتطعت قطيعا من غنم فلان (sihah)؛ القطيع من الغنم جمعه قطعان كالصرمة والفرقة (mufradat)؛ القطيع القطعة من الغنم (maqayis)
- **B017** hızla geride bırakmak — atın diğer atları geçip geride bırakması · çok hızlı tavşan · hızlı hızlı ardı ardına gelenler
  الفرس الجواد يقطع الخيل إذا خلفها ومضى؛ الأرنب السريعة مقطعة النياط؛ جاءت الخيل مقطوطعات أي سراعا بعضها في إثر بعض (ayn)؛ قطع الفرس الخيل تقطيعا أي خلفها ومضى؛ جاءت الخيل مقطوطعات أي سراعا بعضها في إثر بعض (sihah)؛ يقال للأرنب السريعة مقطعة النياط ومقطعة الأسحار؛ الفرس الجواد يقطع الخيل تقطيعا؛ ليس فيكم من تقطع عليه الأعناق مثل أبي بكر (tahdhib)؛ مقطعة النياط الأرنب؛ قطع الفرس الخيل تقطيعا خلفها ومضى؛ جاءت الخيل مقطوطعات أي سراعا (maqayis)
- **B018** benzersiz olmak [kalıp] — belirli nitelikte eşi benzeri olmayan · kötülükte dizginsiz olan
  منقطع القرين في الكرم والسخاء إذا لم يكن له مثل؛ منقطع العقال في الشر والخبث (ayn)؛ فلان منقطع القرين في سخاء أو غيره (sihah)؛ فلان منقطع القرين إذا لم يكن له مثل في سخاء أو فضل (tahdhib)؛ فلان منقطع القرين في سخاء أو غيره (maqayis)
- **B019** yapıca benzer olmak [kalıp] — birine boy ve yapı bakımından benzeyen · düzgün ve güzel beden yapısı
  القطيع شبه النظير؛ هذا قطيع هذا أي شبهه في خلقه وقده؛ حسن التقطيع أي القد (ayn)؛ فلان قطيع فلان أي شبيهه في قده وخلقه؛ شيء حسن التقطيع إذا كان حسن القد (tahdhib)

# s029 Semantic Channel Discovery

## Parent Channels

### 1. P1 (29:1-13) Verification Under Pressure
- Semantic invariant: Pressure makes an inward quality externally legible by testing material, speech, and conduct.
- Surface relation: direct; 29:2-3,10 (ف ت ن), 29:3 (ص د ق), 29:2,10,12 (ق و ل), and 29:10 (ص د ر) stage profession, trial, truth, and what is held in breasts.
- Surprising reach: The passage's epistemic test acquires the concrete mechanics of fire-assay, diagnostic marking, hidden speech, and socially circulating noise.

#### Subchannel A. Fire-Assay and the Mark of Sound Material
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Metal or another material enters heat, changes visibly, and is judged by whether its solidity and promised performance survive.
- Active motifs: assaying metal and persons `quranic:root_001128:B001/m01`; burning and blackening by fire `quranic:root_001128:B002/m01`; material solidity and straightness `quranic:root_000852:B002/m01`; realization of promise in action `quranic:root_000852:B004/m01`; a distinguishing mark `quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`; a covering laid over what is beneath `quranic:root_001307:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ف ت ن 29:2-3,10; ص د ق 29:3; ع ل م 29:3,5-6,8,10-11; ك ف ر 29:7,12.
- Synthesis: Trial is not only adversity but an assay that separates sound from unsound material. Truth is both a hard, straight substance and a promise realized under stress, while knowledge reads the resulting mark through any attempted covering.

#### Subchannel B. Spoken Profession and the Unvoiced Interior
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An utterance leaves the tongue while an unvoiced formulation, intention, and causal source remain within the person.
- Active motifs: articulated speech `quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`; speech held inward before utterance `quranic:root_001272:B012/m01`; inward intention `quranic:root_001533:B013/m01`; the source from which actions issue `quranic:root_000849:B004/m01`; knowledge as disclosure to a knower `quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`; the lying self `quranic:root_001290:B008/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ق و ل 29:2,10,12; ن ف س 29:6; ص د ر 29:10; ع ل م 29:3,5-6,8,10-11; ك ذ ب 29:3,12.
- Synthesis: The professed "we believe" is one layer of a speech event; beneath it sit an unspoken proposition, intention, and the source of subsequent action. Divine knowing therefore reaches the point where language is still interior and distinguishes the lying self before public wording can stabilize its claim.

#### Subchannel C. Injury Amplified as Public Circulation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Harmful speech enters public circulation, becomes reputation or scandal, and escalates into communal disturbance.
- Active motifs: injurious utterance `quranic:root_000023:B001/m01`; talk circulating among people `quranic:root_001272:B007/m01`; public reputation and notoriety `quranic:root_000741:B005/m01`; insulting or shaming speech `quranic:root_000741:B006/m01`; conflict and division among people `quranic:root_001128:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ء ذ ي 29:10; ق و ل 29:2,10,12; س م ع 29:5; ف ت ن 29:2-3,10.
- Synthesis: The "harm concerning God" can be pressured by a social acoustics in which an injury is repeated, acquires notoriety, and becomes factional disturbance. This makes the human trial in 29:10 a mechanism of public amplification rather than an isolated wound.

### 2. P1 (29:1-13) Liability Carried to Settlement
- Semantic invariant: An obligation remains attached to its actor as a load, guarantee, account, and claim until it is weighed and settled.
- Surface relation: direct; 29:12-13 repeatedly joins ح م ل, خ ط ء, and ث ق ل, while 29:7 and 29:13 supply recompense and questioning.
- Surprising reach: Moral burden is materialized as freight, surety, debt collection, and calibrated weight rather than as an abstract quantity.

#### Subchannel A. Load, Surety, and Inseparable Liability
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A visible load is taken up under a guarantee, but the attached liability follows the responsible party and adds weight.
- Active motifs: an external load borne on back, head, mount, or vessel `quranic:root_000357:B001/m01`; entrusted burden or culpability `quranic:root_000357:B003/m01`; surety for damages and obligations `quranic:root_000357:B004/m01`; physical or figurative heaviness `quranic:root_000202:B001/m01`; calibrated weight `quranic:root_000202:B004/m01`; liability that follows its cause `quranic:root_000175:B006/m01`; deliberate offense `quranic:root_000420:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ح م ل 29:12-13; ث ق ل 29:13; ت ب ع 29:12; خ ط ء 29:12.
- Synthesis: The promise to "carry your sins" sounds like a surety agreement, yet liability is not detached freight. It remains a following claim, so the guarantor accumulates another weighted obligation instead of substituting for the original actor.

#### Subchannel B. Account, Claim, and Final Discharge
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Conduct enters an account, a claimant asks for settlement, weight is assessed, and recompense answers the act.
- Active motifs: counting and accounting `quranic:root_000318:B001/m01`; reciprocal recompense for an act `quranic:root_000244:B001/m01`; one thing attempting to stand in for another `quranic:root_000244:B002/m01`; collection and discharge of debt `quranic:root_000244:B003/m01`; asking and claiming `quranic:root_000661:B001/m01`; judicial decision between parties `quranic:root_000348:B002/m01`; intentional work `quranic:root_001046:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ح س ب 29:2,4; ج ز ي 29:7; س ء ل 29:13; ح ك م 29:4; ع م ل 29:4,7-9.
- Synthesis: The local language forms a settlement scene: deeds are counted, claims are heard, substitution is tested, and judgment answers intentional work. Recompense is thus the discharge of a properly attributed account, not a transferable slogan.

### 3. P1 (29:1-13) Tracked Roads and the Appointed Encounter
- Semantic invariant: Directed movement leaves a followable track and terminates at an encounter that cannot be outrun.
- Surface relation: direct; 29:4-5 opposes attempted precedence to the coming term and meeting, while 29:12 commands following a path and 29:8 names return.
- Surprising reach: The moral route becomes a worked road with travelers, ruts, sandal-straps, pursuit, a finish, and an unavoidable reception.

#### Subchannel A. Worked Track, Foot Travelers, and Road Gear
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Walkers follow a worked route by successive traces while footwear and the road's grooves keep bodies aligned to it.
- Active motifs: an extended road `quranic:root_000672:B001/m01`; road users and travelers `quranic:root_000672:B002/m01`; a well-used worked road `quranic:root_001046:B011/m01`; travelers proceeding on foot `quranic:root_001046:B012/m01`; a sandal strap `quranic:root_000791:B004/m01`; road grooves and main tracks `quranic:root_000791:B005/m01`; tracing an effect step by step `quranic:root_000175:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: س ب ل 29:12; ع م ل 29:4,7-9; ش ر ك 29:8; ت ب ع 29:12.
- Synthesis: "Follow our path" is materialized as foot travel on a worked road whose repeated use has cut tracks into it. The sandal strap and road-groove senses make adherence bodily and infrastructural: a route recruits feet through its fitted gear and visible traces.

#### Subchannel B. Race, Finish, and Unavoidable Reception
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A runner tries to gain precedence, but the pursuer closes the distance at a fixed terminus where encounter and return occur.
- Active motifs: precedence in a race `quranic:root_000671:B001/m01`; escaping beyond a pursuer's reach `quranic:root_000671:B004/m01`; two parties meeting face to face `quranic:root_001372:B004/m01`; what one meets of good or harm `quranic:root_001372:B007/m01`; a fixed temporal limit `quranic:root_000016:B001/m01`; arrival at a traveled terminus `quranic:root_000009:B010/m01`; arrival of calamity or death `quranic:root_000009:B011/m01`; return to the point or state of origin `quranic:root_000544:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: س ب ق 29:4; ل ق ي 29:5; ء ج ل 29:5; ء ت ي 29:5; ر ج ع 29:8.
- Synthesis: The attempted escape in 29:4 is a failed race: precedence cannot erase the fixed finish. The coming term is simultaneously a route-end, an arriving event, a face-to-face meeting, and a return.

### 4. P2 (29:14-27) Manufactured Allegiance and Its Reversal
- Semantic invariant: A cultic object becomes powerful through service, possession, affection, and alliance, then those same relations reverse into repudiation and expulsion.
- Surface relation: direct; 29:16-17 and 29:25 join worship, idols, possession, mutual affection, denial, cursing, and absent aid.
- Surprising reach: Idolatry appears less as a static object class than as a deliberately assembled social and property regime whose bonds later run backward.

#### Subchannel A. Idol, Owner, and Servile Hierarchy
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An object is installed as a worshiped superior, people submit in service, and possession and sovereignty make the arrangement durable.
- Active motifs: a made object of worship `quranic:root_000047:B001/m01`; a worshiped idol or stone `quranic:root_001624:B001/m01`; submissive ritual service `quranic:root_000973:B003/m01`; reducing another to servile labor `quranic:root_000973:B004/m01`; ownership and disposal of property `quranic:root_001444:B002/m01`; sovereignty and rule `quranic:root_001444:B003/m01`; an inferior substitute `quranic:root_000502:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ء ل ه 29:16-17,19-20,22-25; و ث ن 29:17,25; ع ب د 29:16-17; م ل ك 29:17; د و ن 29:17,22,25.
- Synthesis: The idols are placed inside an ownership hierarchy: the worshiper behaves as a servant while attributing rule and disposal to an inferior object. The lexical scene exposes the social labor required to manufacture a "master."

#### Subchannel B. Affection as an Alliance Contract
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Shared attachment to a cultic object binds parties through proximity, patronage, and a connective social compact.
- Active motifs: affection and mutual love `quranic:root_001634:B001/m01`; Wadd as a named idol `quranic:root_001634:B005/m01`; loving and protective alliance `quranic:root_001684:B004/m01`; legal clientage `quranic:root_001684:B005/m01`; a connective relation between parties `quranic:root_000170:B003/m01`; taking and possessing an object `quranic:root_000018:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: و د د 29:25; و ل ي 29:22; ب ي ن 29:18,25; ء خ ذ 29:14,25.
- Synthesis: "Affection among you" behaves like alliance infrastructure: proximity, protection, clientage, and possession gather around a shared object. Wadd's lexical identity as an idol sharpens the pun by making affection itself both bond and cultic name.

#### Subchannel C. Repudiation, Separation, and Expulsion
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The alliance is disowned, its connective middle is cut, parties turn away, and each drives the other beyond protection.
- Active motifs: repudiation and disavowal `quranic:root_001307:B005/m01`; separation after connection `quranic:root_000170:B001/m01`; irrevocable severance `quranic:root_000170:B012/m01`; turning away and abandoning proximity `quranic:root_001684:B007/m01`; expulsion under wrath `quranic:root_001359:B001/m01`; reciprocal imprecation `quranic:root_001359:B004/m01`; aid against an adversary `quranic:root_001510:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ك ف ر 29:23,25; ب ي ن 29:18,25; و ل ي 29:22; ل ع ن 29:25; ن ص ر 29:22,25.
- Synthesis: The eschatological reversal dismantles every relation that sustained the idol compact. Affection becomes mutual disavowal, connection becomes irrevocable separation, patronage becomes withdrawal, and blessing is replaced by reciprocal expulsion.

### 5. P2 (29:14-27) The Message Made Visible
- Semantic invariant: A dispatched communication reaches its limit by becoming an intelligible, inspectable mark.
- Surface relation: direct; 29:14,18 name sending and clear delivery, while 29:15,19-20,23-24 repeatedly call for seeing signs and creation.
- Surprising reach: Communication is simultaneously courier-work, eloquent arrival, landmark, banner, and trained observation.

#### Subchannel A. Dispatch, Carriage, and Clear Delivery
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A sender releases a bearer, the bearer carries a consequential report, and expression reaches the intended recipient clearly.
- Active motifs: dispatch and release `quranic:root_000563:B001/m01`; messenger and carried message `quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`; delivery of a message `quranic:root_000151:B002/m01`; reaching an endpoint `quranic:root_000151:B001/m01`; eloquence that reaches its purpose `quranic:root_000151:B004/m01`; spoken clarification `quranic:root_000170:B005/m01`; a consequential report arriving `quranic:root_001464:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ر س ل 29:14,18; ب ل غ 29:18; ب ي ن 29:18,25; ن ب ء 29:27.
- Synthesis: The messenger is a carrier whose task is completed when the communication reaches its limit. "Clear delivery" therefore combines transport and intelligibility: the report arrives and opens its meaning rather than merely occupying the recipient's hearing.

#### Subchannel B. Sign, Banner, and Deliberate Inspection
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A visible mark is raised or distinguished, observers direct eye and judgment toward it, and concealed structure becomes known.
- Active motifs: a manifest sign `quranic:root_000074:B003/m01`; a route-guiding landmark `quranic:root_001040:B002/m02`; an identifying banner `quranic:root_001040:B002/m03`; sensory and inward vision `quranic:root_000531:B001/m01`; a raised banner visible at distance `quranic:root_000531:B011/m01`; directed visual and reflective inspection `quranic:root_001520:B001/m01`; emergence into clarity `quranic:root_000170:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ء ي ي 29:15,23-24; ع ل م 29:15-16; ر ء ي 29:19; ن ظ ر 29:20; ب ي ن 29:18,25.
- Synthesis: The ark, fire-rescue, and recreated world function as raised evidence rather than inert spectacle. A sign is a landmark that solicits disciplined inspection until what was merely visible becomes inwardly known.

### 6. P2 (29:14-27) Origin, Measure, and Return
- Semantic invariant: Making proceeds through prior measure and first construction, then proves its power by turning the made thing back into renewed existence.
- Surface relation: direct; 29:17,19-20 join creating, beginning, redoing, and a later emergence, while 29:21 names reversal toward God.
- Surprising reach: Creation is rendered as leather-cutting, measurement, foundation work, assembly, and a reversible production cycle.

#### Subchannel A. Pre-Measure, First Cut, and Construction
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A maker measures material, determines its limit, opens the work, and establishes the first structural parts.
- Active motifs: measuring before cutting or acting `quranic:root_000434:B001/m01`; originating a made thing `quranic:root_000434:B002/m01`; quantity and limit `quranic:root_001205:B001/m01`; planning by prior measurement `quranic:root_001205:B005/m01`; preparing and fitting a form `quranic:root_001205:B005/m02`; opening and prioritizing the first stage `quranic:root_000090:B001/m01`; producing and bringing into existence `quranic:root_001502:B006/m01`; first members or stones of a basin `quranic:root_001502:B007/m01`
- Ayah anchors: خ ل ق 29:17,19-20; ق د ر 29:20; ب د ء 29:19-20; ن ش ء 29:20.
- Synthesis: The first creation is a workshop sequence: measure precedes cut, opening precedes assembly, and foundational pieces establish the form. The concrete craft sense makes "how creation began" an invitation to inspect fitted production.

#### Subchannel B. Reversal, Return, and Renewed Emergence
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A completed form is turned, sent back toward its point of return, and made to emerge again.
- Active motifs: beginning paired with repetition `quranic:root_000090:B002/m01`; return after departure `quranic:root_001058:B001/m01`; destination of return `quranic:root_001058:B002/m01`; turning a thing from one face to another `quranic:root_001248:B004/m01`; final destination or outcome `quranic:root_001248:B005/m01`; return to an earlier state `quranic:root_000544:B001/m01`; rising and coming forward `quranic:root_001502:B008/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ب د ء 29:19-20; ع و د 29:19; ق ل ب 29:21; ر ج ع 29:17; ن ش ء 29:20.
- Synthesis: Re-creation is not a second unrelated manufacture but a controlled reversal of an existing trajectory. The object is turned, reaches its return-point, and rises into a renewed emergence.

### 7. P2 (29:14-27) Water Governing Survival
- Semantic invariant: Water can encircle and destroy, support a crossing craft, preserve life on raised ground, or descend as apportioned nourishment.
- Surface relation: direct; 29:14-15 gives flood and ship, 29:15 and 29:24 give rescue, and 29:17 makes provision the object of seeking and gratitude.
- Surprising reach: Destruction and sustenance share one hydrological system of enclosure, flotation, elevation, rainfall, and living yield.

#### Subchannel A. Encircling Flood and Worked Floating Craft
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Water surrounds the settlement while shaped timber, roughening tools, and bound floats produce a surface that can cross it.
- Active motifs: overwhelming encircling flood `quranic:root_000957:B002/m01`; a bound raft of skins or timber `quranic:root_000957:B006/m01`; stripping and shaping a surface `quranic:root_000716:B001/m01`; woodworking or roughening tool `quranic:root_000716:B002/m01`; ship cutting the water's face `quranic:root_000716:B003/m01`; swimming or vessel-motion `quranic:root_001063:B001/m01`; a branch-float for crossing water `quranic:root_001063:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ط و ف 29:14; س ف ن 29:15; ع و م 29:14.
- Synthesis: The ship appears as a worked counter-form to encircling water: surfaces are stripped, timber is shaped, and pieces are bound into buoyancy. The same root field that names the flood also supplies the primitive raft that crosses it.

#### Subchannel B. Extraction to Flood-Safe Height
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Persons separate from danger through rapid movement and reach an elevated place beyond the flood-line.
- Active motifs: escape by separation `quranic:root_001476:B001/m01`; rapid departure `quranic:root_001476:B002/m01`; raised ground beyond flood reach `quranic:root_001476:B004/m01`; companionship and close attendance `quranic:root_000844:B001/m01`; preservation through accompaniment `quranic:root_000844:B002/m01`; land as the lower supporting surface `quranic:root_000025:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ن ج و 29:15,24; ص ح ب 29:15; ء ر ض 29:20,22.
- Synthesis: Rescue has a topography: separation from the destructive medium, accelerated movement, and arrival on a raised safe place. Noah's companions and Abraham's rescue are locally joined by extraction that preserves an accompanied group.

#### Subchannel C. Rain-Apportioned Life and Gratitude
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Rain descends as a measured allotment, revives land and animals, and makes a small input visibly productive.
- Active motifs: apportioned gift `quranic:root_000560:B001/m01`; nourishment entering the body `quranic:root_000560:B002/m01`; rain as provision `quranic:root_000560:B003/m01`; land vivified by rain and growth `quranic:root_000383:B002/m01`; gratitude that recognizes a benefactor `quranic:root_000810:B001/m01`; small feed or rain yielding visible increase `quranic:root_000810:B002/m01`; abundant milk after pasture `quranic:root_000810:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ر ز ق 29:17; ح ي ي 29:25; ش ك ر 29:17.
- Synthesis: Provision is not only possessed food; it is rainfall entering a living production system. Gratitude reads the disproportion between a small received input and the greening, fattening, or milk-yield that follows.

### 8. P2 (29:14-27) Generative Continuity
- Semantic invariant: Continuity is produced by scattering and covering viable beginnings, then stabilizing the resulting line through gift, record, and durable return.
- Surface relation: indirect; 29:27 names gifted descendants, prophecy, book, and reward, while 29:17-20 supplies creating and emergence.
- Surprising reach: Genealogy becomes a cultivation process and a documentary act rather than a bare list of heirs.

#### Subchannel A. Seed Scattering, Covering, and Mixed-Age Growth
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Small seed is scattered, covered in soil, rises as fine growth, and remains beside the older stock.
- Active motifs: scattering grain and powder `quranic:root_000511:B002/m01`; fine dispersed emergence `quranic:root_000511:B004/m01`; covering seed with soil `quranic:root_001307:B008/m01`; growing and being raised among a people `quranic:root_001502:B002/m01`; young stock growing beside its source `quranic:root_001624:B004/m01`; lasting and remaining in place `quranic:root_001685:B007/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ذ ر ر 29:27; ك ف ر 29:23,25; ن ش ء 29:20; و ث ن 29:17,25; و ه ب 29:27.
- Synthesis: The descendant line is materialized as cultivation: minute beginnings are distributed, hidden under soil, and raised beside an older stock. Continuity depends on both dispersal and protective covering before it becomes visible growth.

#### Subchannel B. Gift Joined to Record and Vocation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A gift is prepared and made durable, its elements are joined into a record, and a prophetic vocation and return are assigned to it.
- Active motifs: gift without exchange `quranic:root_001685:B001/m01`; preparing a gift for its recipient `quranic:root_001685:B005/m01`; joining parts together `quranic:root_001283:B001/m01`; entering a name in a register or company `quranic:root_001283:B004/m01`; prophetic mediation of a divine report `quranic:root_001464:B003/m01`; recompense returning from work or covenant `quranic:root_000015:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: و ه ب 29:27; ك ت ب 29:27; ن ب ء 29:27; ء ج ر 29:27.
- Synthesis: The gifted line is not left unformed: it is prepared, joined, entered into a durable register, and entrusted with a reporting office. Reward is the returning benefit of that established continuity.

### 9. P3 (29:28-44) Routes and Forums Turned Hostile
- Semantic invariant: Shared channels for movement and speech are converted from public access into mechanisms of obstruction and abuse.
- Surface relation: direct; 29:29 names cutting the road and wrongdoing in the forum, while 29:38 names obstruction from the path despite perceptive capacity.
- Surprising reach: The passage links highway robbery, worked tracks, water access, public council, loud circulation, and the restraint or excess of the tongue.

#### Subchannel A. Cutting the Worked Road
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Travelers move on a known track toward resources until an obstacle, seizure, or exhausted means interrupts passage.
- Active motifs: crossing a distance or water `quranic:root_001240:B003/m01`; a stranded traveler whose means fail `quranic:root_001240:B006/m01`; highway seizure and obstruction `quranic:root_001240:B023/m01`; an extended traversable road `quranic:root_000672:B001/m01`; road users and travelers `quranic:root_000672:B002/m01`; preventing and turning away `quranic:root_000848:B001/m01`; a route leading to water `quranic:root_000848:B004/m01`; a worked road `quranic:root_001046:B011/m01`; walkers on foot `quranic:root_000546:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ق ط ع 29:29; س ب ل 29:29,38; ص د د 29:38; ع م ل 29:38; ر ج ل 29:29.
- Synthesis: Cutting the road is a full disruption of public mobility: a known worked track and its resource route are blocked until the traveler becomes "cut off" through lost mount, food, or money. The lexical assembly makes obstruction an attack on the infrastructure that lets strangers continue.

#### Subchannel B. The Abusive Public Council
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A gathered council raises speech into public noise, circulates shame, and lets verbal reach exceed rational restraint.
- Active motifs: gathered council and consultation `quranic:root_001486:B001/m01`; raised public call `quranic:root_001486:B002/m01`; shame-producing words `quranic:root_001486:B005/m01`; excessive and far-reaching speech `quranic:root_000512:B011/m01`; a prolific speaker `quranic:root_001272:B003/m01`; talk circulating among people `quranic:root_001272:B007/m01`; tongue held back from speech `quranic:root_001036:B003/m01`; an act so hard or strange that it is rejected `quranic:root_001550:B003/m01`; verbal answer and exchange `quranic:root_000273:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ن د و 29:29; ذ ر ع 29:33; ق و ل 29:28-33,36; ع ق ل 29:35,43; ن ك ر 29:29; ج و ب 29:29.
- Synthesis: The forum is not a neutral venue but an amplifier: gathering, raised voice, circulating talk, and shame-producing language create the social force of the act. The restrained tongue of عقل provides the exact counter-operation to a council whose speech has overextended its reach.

### 10. P3 (29:28-44) House, Household, and Failed Support
- Semantic invariant: A dwelling is viable only when fitted structure, inhabited relations, and load-bearing supports work together.
- Surface relation: direct; 29:31-35 repeatedly distinguishes town and household, while 29:38 names dwellings and 29:41 tests protectors through the spider's house.
- Surprising reach: The spider comparison opens into joinery, tent supports, stitched panels, household membership, bodily ribs, hidden pain, and timber-eating decay.

#### Subchannel A. Fitted Dwelling Structure
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A shelter is assembled from a standing support, a fitted socket or joint, transverse members, and joined wall material.
- Active motifs: dwelling and nightly shelter `quranic:root_000166:B001/m01`; crosspieces carrying a house-post `quranic:root_001222:B008/m01`; fitted joint, socket, or door fitting `quranic:root_000347:B011/m01`; standing tool or structural member `quranic:root_001273:B012/m01`; residence as a stable place `quranic:root_000726:B002/m01`; joining two skins or wall-halves `quranic:root_000121:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ب ي ت 29:41; ق ر ي 29:31,34; ح ق ق 29:44; ق و م 29:28-30,35-36; س ك ن 29:38; ب ص ر 29:38.
- Synthesis: The house is a fitted mechanism, not merely an enclosure. Posts, crosspieces, sockets, and joined skins distribute load; the spider-house pressures the comparison by offering the name of shelter without dependable structural work.

#### Subchannel B. Household, Inhabitants, and Welcome
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Kin, residents, and guests are gathered by a dwelling whose social function is recognition, qualification, and safe reception.
- Active motifs: kin and co-inhabitants `quranic:root_000064:B001/m01`; qualification for a role or place `quranic:root_000064:B003/m01`; inhabited familiarity `quranic:root_000064:B004/m01`; household and dependents `quranic:root_000166:B002/m01`; residents of a house `quranic:root_000726:B003/m01`; what gives inward familiarity and calm `quranic:root_000726:B004/m01`; gathered town and townspeople `quranic:root_001222:B001/m01`; hospitality through gathered food `quranic:root_001222:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ء ه ل 29:31-34; ب ي ت 29:41; س ك ن 29:38; ق ر ي 29:31,34.
- Synthesis: "Household" names more than blood descent: it is a relation of residence, qualification, familiarity, and welcome. The repeated negotiation over Lot's family therefore asks who truly belongs inside a protective social dwelling.

#### Subchannel C. Weak Supports and Internal Failure
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A nominal shelter fails because its support is weak, its inner frame hurts, its speech is pretext, and decay consumes its material.
- Active motifs: loss of strength and resolve `quranic:root_001687:B001/m01`; a vulnerable rib or shoulder support `quranic:root_001687:B003/m01`; pain in that support `quranic:root_001687:B004/m01`; empty speech used as a pretext `quranic:root_001687:B008/m01`; a protector responsible for an affair `quranic:root_001684:B003/m01`; protective alliance `quranic:root_001684:B004/m01`; timber-eating termite `quranic:root_000025:B010/m01`; spider-house designation within a fitted joint term `quranic:root_000347:B011/m02`
- Ayah anchors: و ه ن 29:41; و ل ي 29:41; ء ر ض 29:36,39-40,44; ح ق ق 29:44.
- Synthesis: Weakness penetrates every load-bearing register: bodily ribs, household patrons, structural timber, and justificatory speech. The spider-house is weakest not because "house" is meaningless, but because the relations and members that should carry its load do not.

### 11. P3 (29:28-44) Distinct Seizure Mechanics
- Semantic invariant: Each destroyed community is removed by a different physical operation that attacks its assumed stability.
- Surface relation: direct; 29:34,37,40 enumerates descending punishment, tremor, stone-bearing force, cry, sinking, and drowning.
- Surprising reach: The list becomes a mechanics of vibration, projectile wind, immersion, substrate failure, and acoustic shock, with distinct body states and outcomes.

#### Subchannel A. Tremor and Ground-Locked Bodies
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Severe oscillation passes through earth and body until persons lose upright motion and remain pressed to the ground.
- Active motifs: violent shaking across bodies, sea, trees, or thunder `quranic:root_000545:B001/m01`; repeated disturbance and rolling motion `quranic:root_000542:B001/m01`; bodily or terrestrial shudder `quranic:root_000025:B008/m01`; clinging to the ground `quranic:root_000222:B001/m01`; the bodily mass left in place `quranic:root_000222:B003/m01`; becoming fixed in a resulting state `quranic:root_000839:B010/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ر ج ف 29:37; ر ج ز 29:34; ء ر ض 29:36,39-40,44; ج ث م 29:37; ص ب ح 29:37.
- Synthesis: The tremor is a transfer of instability from ground to body. Its outcome is not generic death but loss of stance: bodily masses end adhered to the substrate in the state in which morning finds them.

#### Subchannel B. Stone-Bearing Wind and Descending Impact
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A force moving through the upper air lifts grit and stones, carries them, and delivers repeated impacts downward.
- Active motifs: loose stones and stony ground `quranic:root_000325:B001/m01`; pelting with stones `quranic:root_000325:B002/m01`; violent wind carrying grit or hail `quranic:root_000325:B003/m01`; upper cover, sky, and cloud `quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`; causing an affliction to descend `quranic:root_001492:B002/m01`; a calamity that comes down upon a people `quranic:root_001492:B006/m01`; weather striking land and vegetation `quranic:root_000906:B013/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ح ص ب 29:40; س م و 29:34,44; ن ز ل 29:34; ض ر ب 29:43.
- Synthesis: The stone-bearing punishment has an aerial transport mechanism: wind entrains particles, the sky becomes a delivery field, and impacts descend onto exposed ground. It is therefore distinct from both immersion and substrate collapse.

#### Subchannel C. Immersion and Engulfing Water
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Water fills the available space, occupies the victim's field, and submerges until movement and life cease.
- Active motifs: sinking beneath engulfing water `quranic:root_001080:B001/m01`; land or eye filled with water `quranic:root_001080:B003/m01`; a medium occupying the whole field `quranic:root_001080:B005/m01`; a basin or depression retaining water `quranic:root_000018:B006/m01`; the gathered body of water `quranic:root_000222:B007/m01`; lower terrestrial support `quranic:root_000025:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: غ ر ق 29:40; ء خ ذ 29:37,40-41; ج ث م 29:37; ء ر ض 29:36,39-40,44.
- Synthesis: Drowning is modeled as progressive occupation: water gathers, fills, and takes over the victim's whole field. Unlike the tremor, which arrests bodies on the surface, immersion removes the breathable surface itself.

#### Subchannel D. Substrate Failure and Downward Disappearance
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Supporting ground softens or opens, the victim is forced below its surface, and the former dwelling becomes an encircling adverse state.
- Active motifs: ground swallowing and hiding what enters it `quranic:root_000410:B001/m01`; imposed humiliation or hardship `quranic:root_000410:B006/m01`; terrestrial supporting surface `quranic:root_000025:B001/m01`; heaviness and adhesion to ground `quranic:root_000025:B006/m01`; the adverse circle closing around a people `quranic:root_000499:B004/m01`; bodily condition taking hold `quranic:root_000018:B007/m01`
- Ayah anchors: خ س ف 29:40; ء ر ض 29:36,39-40,44; د و ر 29:37; ء خ ذ 29:37,40-41.
- Synthesis: Sinking attacks the support relation itself: what appeared load-bearing becomes the medium of disappearance. The lexical field also carries humiliation, so spatial descent and loss of standing reinforce one another without collapsing into the other punishments.

#### Subchannel E. Acoustic Shock and Immediate Arrest
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A sudden raised cry enters as an alarm or punitive blast and immediately arrests the inhabitants in their dwelling.
- Active motifs: high, penetrating cry `quranic:root_000895:B001/m01`; terrifying punitive blast `quranic:root_000895:B002/m01`; disturbance carried by sound `quranic:root_000545:B001/m02`; body fixed against the ground `quranic:root_000222:B001/m01`; adverse condition encircling a dwelling `quranic:root_000499:B004/m01`; first light exposing the completed state `quranic:root_000839:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ص ي ح 29:40; ر ج ف 29:37; ج ث م 29:37; د و ر 29:37; ص ب ح 29:37.
- Synthesis: The cry is an acoustic event rather than a wind, flood, or collapse. Its force is measured by abrupt state-change: raised sound becomes alarm and then silence, with daylight revealing bodies no longer able to leave the dwelling.

### 12. P3 (29:28-44) Examples Made Legible
- Semantic invariant: A truth becomes cognitively usable when it is marked, modeled, and impressed into a form that can restrain mistaken judgment.
- Surface relation: direct; 29:35 and 29:44 call remains and creation signs, while 29:41-43 explicitly gives a likeness, strikes examples, and limits understanding to knowers.
- Surprising reach: Parable-making behaves like heraldry, model-making, molding, and tethering: cognition is given a durable form and then held to it.

#### Subchannel A. Mark, Landmark, and Inward Sight
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A distinguishing trace remains in the landscape, directs attention, and becomes inward recognition rather than bare looking.
- Active motifs: manifest sign `quranic:root_000074:B003/m01`; retained route-guiding landmark `quranic:root_001040:B002/m02`; emergence into visible clarity `quranic:root_000170:B004/m01`; gestural clarification `quranic:root_000170:B005/m02`; inward sight and evidentiary understanding `quranic:root_000121:B002/m01`; retained trace after departure `quranic:root_000180:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ء ي ي 29:35,44; ع ل م 29:28,32,41-43; ب ي ن 29:35,38-39; ب ص ر 29:38; ت ر ك 29:35.
- Synthesis: The "clear sign" left from the town is a trace that functions like a landmark: it first catches sight, then opens meaning, then becomes inward evidence. Remains are useful because they preserve direction after the event itself has departed.

#### Subchannel B. Struck Model and Rational Restraint
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A maker strikes or stamps a representative form so that observers can compare structures and bind judgment to the demonstrated relation.
- Active motifs: proverbial formulation `quranic:root_001397:B003/m01`; represented model or image `quranic:root_001397:B008/m01`; example serving as an instructive sign `quranic:root_001397:B011/m01`; rendering an example explicit `quranic:root_000906:B003/m01`; type or mold for repeated form `quranic:root_000906:B007/m01`; striking tool `quranic:root_000906:B014/m02`; craft of striking `quranic:root_000906:B014/m03`; reason that restrains ignorance `quranic:root_001036:B001/m01`; tethering a moving animal `quranic:root_001036:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: م ث ل 29:41,43; ض ر ب 29:43; ع ق ل 29:35,43.
- Synthesis: To "strike" an example is to make a repeatable model whose form carries an invariant relation. عقل contributes both understanding and tether: the comparison checks wandering judgment by fastening it to a visible structure.

### 13. P4 (29:45-56) Revelation From Hidden Signal to Public Text
- Semantic invariant: Revelation moves from concealed delivery through inscription and ordered sequence into voiced remembrance and embodied knowledge.
- Surface relation: direct; 29:45-51 repeatedly joins revelation, book, recitation, writing, signs, breasts, and remembrance.
- Surprising reach: The text is simultaneously a hidden signal, stone inscription, joined document, following voice, mnemonic recovery, and archive carried in the chest.

#### Subchannel A. Hidden Delivery and Durable Inscription
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Knowledge is conveyed discreetly, impressed into a durable surface, joined as a document, and delivered into its proper place.
- Active motifs: concealed communication of knowledge `quranic:root_001633:B001/m01`; writing and engraving `quranic:root_001633:B003/m01`; divine report and inspiration `quranic:root_001633:B004/m01`; revelation delivered downward `quranic:root_001492:B002/m02`; ordered placement into the proper rank `quranic:root_001492:B004/m01`; written document and arranged letters `quranic:root_001283:B002/m01`; binding determination in writing `quranic:root_001283:B003/m01`; extended written line `quranic:root_000422:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: و ح ي 29:45; ن ز ل 29:46-47,50-51; ك ت ب 29:45-48,51; خ ط ط 29:48.
- Synthesis: Revelation traverses two media without losing identity: a hidden transfer of knowledge becomes a durable inscription. Descent is also placement, so the book arrives not merely from above but into an ordered textual form.

#### Subchannel B. Following Voice and Recovered Presence
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A reader follows a prior sequence, voice answers voice, and repeated naming restores what could otherwise pass out of active memory.
- Active motifs: following in ordered succession `quranic:root_000186:B001/m01`; a voice following another voice `quranic:root_000186:B007/m01`; inward recovery after absence `quranic:root_000516:B003/m01`; voiced naming and mention `quranic:root_000516:B004/m01`; reminder that restores presence `quranic:root_000516:B009/m01`; articulated utterance `quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`; clarification through speech `quranic:root_000170:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ت ل و 29:45,48,51; ذ ك ر 29:45,51; ق و ل 29:46,50,52,55; ب ي ن 29:49-50,52.
- Synthesis: Recitation is lexical following: the present voice walks behind an established sequence and can be answered by another voice. Remembrance keeps that chain active by repeatedly restoring the text to heart and tongue.

#### Subchannel C. Signs Archived in Breasts
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Distinguishing signs are held in the chest as known evidence and can be produced as testimony when challenged.
- Active motifs: visible sign `quranic:root_000074:B003/m01`; disclosure to the knower `quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`; distinguishing mark `quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`; chest as bodily container `quranic:root_000849:B001/m01`; source from which action and expression issue `quranic:root_000849:B004/m01`; testimony that declares known fact `quranic:root_000822:B002/m01`; sign that witnesses a condition `quranic:root_000822:B008/m01`; fine awareness `quranic:root_000798:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ء ي ي 29:47,49-50; ع ل م 29:45,49,52; ص د ر 29:49; ش ه د 29:52; ش ع ر 29:53.
- Synthesis: The signs are not only external marks; they are stored in the breasts of those given knowledge. This turns the body into an archive whose contents can emerge as informed testimony, while lack of شعور names failure to access even fine-grained evidence.

### 14. P4 (29:45-56) Prayer as Established Restraint
- Semantic invariant: Prayer establishes a bodily and social order whose operation is to stop conduct at the boundary of the shameful and unrecognized.
- Surface relation: direct; 29:45 commands recitation and establishment of prayer, then states that prayer restrains indecency and wrong.
- Surprising reach: Ritual becomes a standing structure, directional alignment, cognitive brake, weaning process, and endpoint that makes further pursuit unnecessary.

#### Subchannel A. Standing, Direction, and Maintained Service
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A worshiper takes an upright position, maintains the rite, aligns to its direction, and submits the body in service.
- Active motifs: prescribed prayer with standing and prostration `quranic:root_000879:B003/m01`; houses and places of prayer `quranic:root_000879:B007/m01`; bodily standing `quranic:root_001273:B002/m01`; sustained care and maintenance `quranic:root_001273:B004/m01`; straight alignment `quranic:root_001273:B008/m01`; submissive worship `quranic:root_000973:B003/m01`; prayer-direction faced by the body `quranic:root_001198:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ص ل و 29:45; ق و م 29:45,51; ع ب د 29:56; ق ب ل 29:48.
- Synthesis: "Establish prayer" combines erection and maintenance: the body stands, the practice is kept in repair, and attention is aligned toward a direction. Prayer thereby supplies an ordered stance before it supplies a prohibitive effect.

#### Subchannel B. Braking, Weaning, and Ending the Wrong
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Reason and prohibition arrest an act, detach appetite from it, and terminate the search for further indulgence.
- Active motifs: prohibition and active restraint `quranic:root_001560:B001/m01`; reason that prevents ugliness `quranic:root_001560:B003/m01`; sufficiency that ends further seeking `quranic:root_001560:B005/m01`; overt indecency in speech or conduct `quranic:root_001134:B003/m01`; rejected ugliness `quranic:root_001550:B006/m01`; preventing or weaning from an object `quranic:root_000994:B003/m01`; ugly conduct exceeding its measure `quranic:root_001134:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ن ه ي 29:45; ف ح ش 29:45; ن ك ر 29:45; ع ذ ب 29:53-55.
- Synthesis: Prayer's restraint is not a vague moral influence. Its lexical mechanism is a brake that reason applies, a weaning from appetite, and an endpoint after which the act no longer solicits pursuit.

### 15. P4 (29:45-56) Appointed Arrival and Total Enclosure
- Semantic invariant: What is demanded prematurely still arrives only at its fixed time, then occupies every available direction.
- Surface relation: direct; 29:53-55 joins demands for haste, a named term, sudden arrival, surrounding punishment, and coverage from above and beneath the feet.
- Surprising reach: Temporal impatience and spatial enclosure become one trap: an event held to its schedule becomes inescapable once released.

#### Subchannel A. Haste Against the Fixed Time
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A claimant urges an event forward, but its appointed limit holds until the event arrives unexpectedly and with destructive force.
- Active motifs: fixed endpoint in time `quranic:root_000016:B001/m01`; premature acceleration `quranic:root_000987:B001/m01`; short or rapid route `quranic:root_000987:B008/m01`; surprise without anticipation `quranic:root_000135:B001/m01`; arrival and occurrence `quranic:root_000009:B001/m01`; arrival of destruction `quranic:root_000009:B011/m01`; coming into presence `quranic:root_000281:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ء ج ل 29:53; ع ج ل 29:53-54; ب غ ت 29:53; ء ت ي 29:47,49,53; ج ي ء 29:53.
- Synthesis: Demanding haste cannot shorten the assigned duration. The event remains held at its endpoint, then crosses from expected future into sudden presence with no interval left for adjustment.

#### Subchannel B. Enclosure From Above and Below
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A surrounding force closes the perimeter, covers awareness, and applies punitive pressure from both vertical directions.
- Active motifs: physical encirclement `quranic:root_000372:B001/m01`; overpowering enclosure ending in ruin `quranic:root_000372:B006/m01`; upper spatial direction `quranic:root_001188:B001/m01`; domination from above `quranic:root_001188:B003/m01`; lower direction beneath the feet `quranic:root_000177:B001/m01`; calamity that covers everyone `quranic:root_001088:B002/m01`; punishment and severe pain `quranic:root_000994:B005/m01`; experiential tasting of what descends `quranic:root_000526:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ح و ط 29:54; ف و ق 29:55; ت ح ت 29:55; غ ش و 29:55; ع ذ ب 29:53-55; ذ و ق 29:55.
- Synthesis: The punishment is spatially complete: perimeter, upper field, and lower footing all become active. "Taste" adds inward experience to outward enclosure, so there is no untouched position from which the event can remain merely observed.

### 16. P4 (29:45-56) Argument Without Rupture
- Semantic invariant: Disagreement can be tightly reasoned while preserving peace, shared orientation, and the interlocutor's human belonging.
- Surface relation: direct; 29:46 commands disputation only in the best manner, excepts active wrongdoers, and ends with one God and shared submission.
- Surprising reach: Argument is a braided cord whose strength can either hold relation together or become a wrestling grip; the best form keeps unity without erasing difference.

#### Subchannel A. Tightly Woven but Excellent Exchange
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Two speakers review claims in a tightly wound exchange while disciplined excellence prevents verbal contest from becoming bodily overthrow.
- Active motifs: tightly twisting cord or woven material `quranic:root_000229:B001/m01`; argumentative exchange and verbal contest `quranic:root_000229:B002/m01`; throwing an opponent to hard ground `quranic:root_000229:B003/m01`; negotiation in an affair `quranic:root_001272:B009/m01`; what is beautiful rather than ugly `quranic:root_000323:B001/m01`; excellent action exceeding bare justice `quranic:root_000323:B002/m01`; interlocutors joined by religious or communal belonging `quranic:root_000064:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: ج د ل 29:46; ق و ل 29:46,50,52,55; ح س ن 29:46; ء ه ل 29:46.
- Synthesis: Debate is structurally tense like a twisted cord: its turns can strengthen a relation or be used to throw an opponent down. The "best" mode preserves tight reasoning while adding generosity beyond the minimum owed to an interlocutor.

#### Subchannel B. Peaceful Unity With Distinct Parties
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Distinct communities remain separate participants yet converge on one object of worship and a non-hostile relation.
- Active motifs: soundness free of injury `quranic:root_000737:B001/m01`; peace opposed to war `quranic:root_000737:B004/m01`; handing oneself or an affair over `quranic:root_000737:B012/m01`; divine unity without partner `quranic:root_001631:B004/m01`; different parties united in one respect `quranic:root_001631:B005/m01`; worship directed to the deity `quranic:root_000047:B001/m01`; trusting assent `quranic:root_000054:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: س ل م 29:46; و ح د 29:46; ء ل ه 29:45-46,50,52; ء م ن 29:46-47,51-52,56.
- Synthesis: Unity here is not social erasure but convergence "in one respect": one deity and a shared posture of handing oneself over. Peace safeguards the distinct participants so that common worship can be affirmed without coercive fusion.
